Nemychnikova,Vedant Nanda,Victor Jouault,Vincent Maladière,Vincent Pfister,Virgile Richard,Vladislav Bataev,Wassim Bouaziz,Wen-Ding Li,William Havard,William Marshall,Xinghui Li,Xingran Guo,Xinyu Yang,Yannic Neuhaus,Yassine El Ouahidi,Yassir Bendou,Yihan Wang,Yimu Pan,Zaccharie Ramzi,Zhenlin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Voxtral TTS是一个表达性多语言文本转语音模型，仅需3秒参考音频即可生成自然语音，采用混合架构结合自回归语义令牌生成和流匹配声学令牌，在人类评估中优于ElevenLabs Flash v2.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个能够从极少量参考音频（仅3秒）生成自然、表达性强的多语言语音的文本转语音系统，解决现有模型在语音克隆自然度和表达性方面的不足。

Method: 采用混合架构：1）自回归生成语义语音令牌；2）流匹配生成声学令牌。使用Voxtral Codec语音令牌化器，采用混合VQ-FSQ量化方案从头训练。支持多语言语音克隆。

Result: 在由母语者进行的人类评估中，Voxtral TTS在自然度和表达性方面优于ElevenLabs Flash v2.5，获得68.4%的胜率。模型权重以CC BY-NC许可证发布。

Conclusion: Voxtral TTS是一个高效的多语言语音克隆系统，仅需3秒参考音频即可生成高质量表达性语音，在人类评估中表现优异，为语音合成领域提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: We introduce Voxtral TTS, an expressive multilingual text-to-speech model that generates natural speech from as little as 3 seconds of reference audio. Voxtral TTS adopts a hybrid architecture that combines auto-regressive generation of semantic speech tokens with flow-matching for acoustic tokens. These tokens are encoded and decoded with Voxtral Codec, a speech tokenizer trained from scratch with a hybrid VQ-FSQ quantization scheme. In human evaluations conducted by native speakers, Voxtral TTS is preferred for multilingual voice cloning due to its naturalness and expressivity, achieving a 68.4\% win rate over ElevenLabs Flash v2.5. We release the model weights under a CC BY-NC license.

</details>


### [103] [Agent Factories for High Level Synthesis: How Far Can General-Purpose Coding Agents Go in Hardware Optimization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25719)
*Abhishek Bhandwaldar,Mihir Choudhury,Ruchir Puri,Akash Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体协同优化硬件设计：通过两阶段流水线，将设计分解为子内核独立优化，再通过专家智能体探索跨函数优化，实现硬件设计自动优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用编码智能体（无需硬件特定训练）在多大程度上能够从高级算法规范优化硬件设计，研究智能体规模化是否可作为高层次综合（HLS）优化的实用有效维度

Method: 提出智能体工厂两阶段流水线：第一阶段将设计分解为子内核，独立优化每个子内核（使用pragma和代码级转换），并构建整数线性规划（ILP）在面积约束下组装全局有前景的配置；第二阶段在顶级ILP解决方案上启动N个专家智能体，探索跨函数优化（如pragma重组、循环融合、内存重构等）

Result: 在HLS-Eval和Rodinia-HLS的12个内核上评估，使用Claude Code和AMD Vitis HLS。智能体数量从1扩展到10时，平均获得8.27倍加速，较难基准测试表现更佳：streamcluster超过20倍，kmeans约10倍。智能体无需领域特定训练即可重新发现已知硬件优化模式，最佳设计通常不来自顶级ILP候选方案

Conclusion: 智能体规模化是HLS优化的实用有效维度，全局优化能发现子内核搜索遗漏的改进，通用编码智能体能够有效优化硬件设计而无需硬件特定训练

Abstract: We present an empirical study of how far general-purpose coding agents -- without hardware-specific training -- can optimize hardware designs from high-level algorithmic specifications. We introduce an agent factory, a two-stage pipeline that constructs and coordinates multiple autonomous optimization agents.
  In Stage~1, the pipeline decomposes a design into sub-kernels, independently optimizes each using pragma and code-level transformations, and formulates an Integer Linear Program (ILP) to assemble globally promising configurations under an area constraint. In Stage~2, it launches $N$ expert agents over the top ILP solutions, each exploring cross-function optimizations such as pragma recombination, loop fusion, and memory restructuring that are not captured by sub-kernel decomposition.
  We evaluate the approach on 12 kernels from HLS-Eval and Rodinia-HLS using Claude Code (Opus~4.5/4.6) with AMD Vitis HLS. Scaling from 1 to 10 agents yields a mean $8.27\times$ speedup over baseline, with larger gains on harder benchmarks: streamcluster exceeds $20\times$ and kmeans reaches approximately $10\times$. Across benchmarks, agents consistently rediscover known hardware optimization patterns without domain-specific training, and the best designs often do not originate from top-ranked ILP candidates, indicating that global optimization exposes improvements missed by sub-kernel search. These results establish agent scaling as a practical and effective axis for HLS optimization.

</details>


### [104] [R-C2: Cycle-Consistent Reinforcement Learning Improves Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25720)
*Zirui Zhang,Haoyu Dong,Kexin Pei,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RC2：通过强化学习框架利用跨模态不一致性作为学习信号，通过循环一致性约束提升多模态模型的推理准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在处理同一概念的视觉和文本表示时经常产生矛盾预测，这种跨模态不一致性通常被视为失败。然而，作者认为这种不一致性实际上提供了丰富的自然学习信号，而不是需要掩盖的问题。

Method: 提出RC2强化学习框架，通过强制跨模态循环一致性来解决内部冲突。要求模型执行反向推理、切换模态，并通过前向推理可靠地重建答案，从而获得密集的无标签奖励。这种循环约束促使模型自主对齐其内部表示。

Result: 优化这种结构能够减轻模态特定错误，并将推理准确性提升高达7.6个百分点。这表明高级推理不仅来自数据扩展，还来自对世界的结构一致性理解。

Conclusion: 跨模态不一致性可以作为有价值的学习信号，通过循环一致性约束能够有效提升多模态模型的内部表示对齐和推理能力，为构建更稳健的感知系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robust perception and reasoning require consistency across sensory modalities. Yet current multimodal models often violate this principle, yielding contradictory predictions for visual and textual representations of the same concept. Rather than masking these failures with standard voting mechanisms, which can amplify systematic biases, we show that cross-modal inconsistency provides a rich and natural signal for learning. We introduce RC2, a reinforcement learning framework that resolves internal conflicts by enforcing cross-modal cycle consistency. By requiring a model to perform backward inference, switch modalities, and reliably reconstruct the answer through forward inference, we obtain a dense, label-free reward. This cyclic constraint encourages the model to align its internal representations autonomously. Optimizing for this structure mitigates modality-specific errors and improves reasoning accuracy by up to 7.6 points. Our results suggest that advanced reasoning emerges not only from scaling data, but also from enforcing a structurally consistent understanding of the world.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Response-Aware Risk-Constrained Control Barrier Function With Application to Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24598)
*Qijun Liao,Jue Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于响应感知风险约束控制屏障函数的统一控制框架，用于车辆动态安全边界控制，解决物理模型参数失配问题，通过CVaR理论将确定性安全约束转化为概率约束，实现安全与跟踪性能的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对车辆动态安全边界控制中物理模型参数失配（特别是路面附着系数在线准确估计困难）的问题，传统方法在极端条件下会出现输出发散现象，需要一种能自适应调整安全边界、减少性能损失的控制框架。

Method: 1. 构建不确定性传播模型，融合名义动力学先验与直接车辆响应；2. 使用CVaR理论将确定性安全约束转化为屏障函数导数的尾部风险概率约束；3. 基于逆Wishart先验的贝叶斯在线学习机制实时识别环境噪声协方差；4. 结合控制Lyapunov函数构建统一的二阶锥规划控制器。

Result: 高保真动力学仿真表明：在极端条件下，该方法消除了传统方法的输出发散现象，实现了安全与跟踪性能的帕累托改进。对于选定的风险水平，每步安全违反概率理论上有界于约2%，所有测试场景中均未观察到边界违反。

Conclusion: 该框架通过响应感知的风险约束控制屏障函数，有效解决了模型参数失配问题，实现了自适应安全边界调整，在保证理论安全概率边界的同时提升了控制性能，为车辆动态安全控制提供了统一且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a unified control framework based on Response-Aware Risk-Constrained Control Barrier Function for dynamic safety boundary control of vehicles. Addressing the problem of physical model parameter mismatch, the framework constructs an uncertainty propagation model that fuses nominal dynamics priors with direct vehicle body responses. Utilizing simplified single-track dynamics to provide a baseline direction for control gradients and covering model deviations through statistical analysis of body response signals, the framework eliminates the dependence on accurate online estimation of road surface adhesion coefficients. By introducing Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) theory, the framework reformulates traditional deterministic safety constraints into probabilistic constraints on the tail risk of barrier function derivatives. Combined with a Bayesian online learning mechanism based on inverse Wishart priors, it identifies environmental noise covariance in real-time, adaptively tuning safety margins to reduce performance loss under prior parameter mismatch. Finally, based on Control Lyapunov Function (CLF), a unified Second-Order Cone Programming (SOCP) controller is constructed. Theoretical analysis establishes convergence of Sequential Convex Programming to local Karush-Kuhn-Tucker points and provides per-step probabilistic safety bounds. High-fidelity dynamics simulations demonstrate that under extreme conditions, the method not only eliminates the output divergence phenomenon of traditional methods but also achieves Pareto improvement in both safety and tracking performance. For the chosen risk level, the per-step safety violation probability is theoretically bounded by approximately 2%, validated through high-fidelity simulations showing zero boundary violations across all tested scenarios.

</details>


### [106] [Period-aware asymptotic gain with application to a periodically forced synchronization circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24600)
*Anton Ponomarev,Lutz Gröll,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的周期感知渐近增益(PAG)，利用输入信号的周期性信息，相比传统渐近增益(AG)能提供更精确的输出渐近估计，并能量化带宽、谐振行为和高频阻尼等特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渐近增益(AG)仅基于输入边界估计输出渐近界，当输入具有周期性特征时，这种估计不够精确。需要利用周期性信息来获得更精确的渐近估计，并能量化系统的频率相关特性。

Method: 在系统收敛到周期解的前提下，引入周期感知渐近增益(PAG)概念。该增益利用输入信号的周期性信息，能够区分短周期（高频）和长周期（低频）信号，从而提供更精确的渐近估计。

Result: PAG能够严格量化系统的带宽、谐振行为和高频阻尼等频率相关特性。论文讨论了PAG的计算方法，并通过电力电子领域的数值示例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 周期感知渐近增益(PAG)是对传统渐近增益(AG)的重要扩展，能够利用输入信号的周期性信息提供更精确的输出渐近估计，并能量化系统的频率响应特性，具有理论和应用价值。

Abstract: The classical asymptotic gain (AG) is a concept known from the input-to-state stability theory. Given a uniform input bound, AG estimates the asymptotic bound of the output. Sometimes, however, more information is known about the input than just a bound. In this paper we consider the case of a periodic input. Under the assumption that the system converges to a periodic solution, we introduce a new gain, called period-aware asymptotic gain (PAG), which employs periodicity to enable a sharper asymptotic estimation of the output. Since the PAG can distinguish between short-period ("high-frequency") and long-period ("low-frequency") signals, it is able to rigorously quantify such properties as bandwidth, resonant behavior, and high-frequency damping. We discuss how the PAG can be computed and illustrate it with a numerical example from the field of power electronics.

</details>


### [107] [Cone-Induced Geometry and Sampling for Determinantal PSD-Weighted Graph Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24913)
*Papri Dey*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了行列式PSD加权图模型，提出了基于仿射不变log-det度量的参数空间几何结构，并开发了适用于锥支撑采样的几何感知MCMC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究行列式正半定加权图模型的几何结构，为这类模型提供数学基础的计算框架，解决锥支撑参数空间的采样问题。

Method: 1) 在PSD参数空间上建立基于仿射不变log-det度量的自然几何；2) 通过秩一探测和有限差分曲率校准验证几何结构；3) 使用相同度量定义内在Gibbs目标和几何感知的Metropolis-adjusted Langevin提议；4) 在对称正定设置下开发显式采样器。

Result: 1) 模型具有显式方向导数、Rayleigh型分解和度量的拉回；2) 几何结构能准确排序局部敏感扰动方向；3) 在对称正定设置下，采样器显式且比朴素欧几里得漂移基线更高效；4) 建立了从行列式PSD图模型到内在几何和实用锥感知采样的完整计算流程。

Conclusion: 该研究为行列式PSD图模型提供了具体的、数学基础的计算框架，将模型、内在几何和锥感知采样有机结合，提高了采样效率并建立了完整的理论-计算管道。

Abstract: We study determinantal PSD-weighted graph models in which edge parameters lie in a product positive semidefinite cone and the block graph Laplacian generates the log-det energy \[ Φ(W)=-\log\det(L(W)+R). \] The model admits explicit directional derivatives, a Rayleigh-type factorization, and a pullback of the affine-invariant log-det metric, yielding a natural geometry on the PSD parameter space. In low PSD dimension, we validate this geometry through rank-one probing and finite-difference curvature calibration, showing that it accurately ranks locally sensitive perturbation directions. We then use the same metric to define intrinsic Gibbs targets and geometry-aware Metropolis-adjusted Langevin proposals for cone-supported sampling. In the symmetric positive definite setting, the resulting sampler is explicit and improves sampling efficiency over a naive Euclidean-drift baseline under the same target law. These results provide a concrete, mathematically grounded computational pipeline from determinantal PSD graph models to intrinsic geometry and practical cone-aware sampling.

</details>


### [108] [New Characterizations of Nonsmooth Convex Functions via Generalized Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24951)
*Vo Thanh Phat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究非光滑扩展实值弱凸函数的凸性性质，建立使用二阶广义导数工具（包括次梯度图形导数、二阶次导数和二阶次微分）的凸性新刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱凸函数在现代优化及其应用中具有核心地位，但非光滑框架下的凸性刻画需要更深入的二阶分析工具。

Method: 使用二阶广义导数工具：次梯度图形导数、二阶次导数和二阶次微分，推导非光滑框架下凸性的必要和充分条件。

Result: 建立了非光滑扩展实值弱凸函数凸性的新刻画，提供了基于二阶广义导数工具的凸性判别条件。

Conclusion: 通过二阶广义导数工具可以有效地刻画非光滑弱凸函数的凸性性质，为现代优化理论提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: This paper studies the convexity properties of nonsmooth extended-real-valued weakly convex functions, a class of functions that is central to modern optimization and its applications. We establish new characterizations of convexity using second-order generalized derivative tools, including subgradient graphical derivatives, second subderivatives, and second-order subdifferentials. These tools allow us to derive necessary and sufficient conditions for convexity in the nonsmooth framework.

</details>


### [109] [Optimization of Closed-Loop Shallow Geothermal Systems Using Analytical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24957)
*Oliver Heinzel,Smajil Halilovic,Thomas Hamacher,Michael Ulbrich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑流体温度限制的闭环浅层地热系统优化设计方法，用于确定垂直埋管换热器的最佳深度和空间布置


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环浅层地热系统是住宅供暖和制冷领域脱碳的关键技术，但在规划阶段需要找到垂直埋管换热器的最佳设计（包括深度和空间布置），同时考虑热载体流体的温度限制等约束条件

Method: 采用有限线源模型模拟地下温度变化，结合钻孔内热传输的解析模型，开发了一种考虑温度限制约束的BHE场优化设计新方法

Result: 该方法在实际场景中得到了验证，预计将改进当前BHE系统的规划和设计实践

Conclusion: 提出的优化设计方法能够有效考虑规划阶段的监管实践要求，为闭环浅层地热系统的优化设计提供了实用工具

Abstract: Closed-loop shallow geothermal systems are one of the key technologies for decarbonizing the residential heating and cooling sector. The primary type of these systems involves vertical borehole heat exchangers (BHEs). During the planning phase, it is essential to find the optimal design for these systems, including the depth and spatial arrangement of the BHEs. In this work, we have developed a novel approach to find the optimal design of BHE fields, taking into account constraints such as temperature limits of the heat carrier fluid. These limits correspond to the regulatory practices applied during the planning phase. The approach uses a finite line source model to simulate temperature changes in the ground in combination with an analytical model of heat transport within the boreholes. Our approach is demonstrated using realistic scenarios and is expected to improve current practice in the planning and design of BHE systems.

</details>


### [110] [The Value of Information in Resource-Constrained Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24974)
*Ruicheng Ao,Jiashuo Jiang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究在容量约束下，需求预测不确定性如何影响动态定价决策，提出认证预测和替代模型两种机制来改善定价性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在定价易逝资源（如机票、酒店房间）时使用需求预测，但预测质量参差不齐。在硬容量约束下，基于不准确预测的决策会不可逆地耗尽未来所需库存，需要研究预测不确定性如何影响动态定价决策

Method: 研究线性需求、随机噪声和有限容量下的动态定价问题。提出两种机制：1）具有已知误差界的认证需求预测，确定系统运行区域；2）与真实需求相关但有偏的替代模型，通过控制变量减少学习方差。算法基于边界吸引机制，在退化容量边界附近稳定定价

Result: 认证预测将遗憾从O(√T)降低到O(log T)，当误差界ε⁰≲T^{-1/4}时，证明该阈值是紧的。替代模型通过控制变量将学习方差减少(1-ρ²)倍。两种机制可组合：预测确定遗憾机制，替代模型在其中收紧估计

Conclusion: 预测不确定性显著影响容量约束下的动态定价性能。认证预测和替代模型是互补机制，分别通过确定运行区域和减少方差来改善定价决策。边界吸引机制提供了无需非退化假设的稳健解决方案

Abstract: Firms that price perishable resources -- airline seats, hotel rooms, seasonal inventory -- now routinely use demand predictions, but these predictions vary widely in quality. Under hard capacity constraints, acting on an inaccurate prediction can irreversibly deplete inventory needed for future periods. We study how prediction uncertainty propagates into dynamic pricing decisions with linear demand, stochastic noise, and finite capacity. A certified demand forecast with known error bound~$ε^0$ specifies where the system should operate: it shifts regret from $O(\sqrt{T})$ to $O(\log T)$ when $ε^0 \lesssim T^{-1/4}$, and we prove this threshold is tight. A misspecified surrogate model -- biased but correlated with true demand -- cannot set prices directly but reduces learning variance by a factor of $(1-ρ^2)$ through control variates. The two mechanisms compose: the forecast determines the regret regime; the surrogate tightens estimation within it. All algorithms rest on a boundary attraction mechanism that stabilizes pricing near degenerate capacity boundaries without requiring non-degeneracy assumptions. Experiments confirm the phase transition threshold, the variance reduction from surrogates, and robustness across problem instances.

</details>


### [111] [Vertical Contracts for Safety Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25246)
*Armin Pirastehzad,Bart Besselink*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于契约形式化的方法，将连续时间安全控制问题转化为离散时间问题，利用离散时间的高效求解方案解决连续时间安全控制难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间安全控制问题通常难以直接处理，而离散时间控制问题存在大量高效求解方案。本文旨在利用契约形式化，将连续时间安全控制问题转化为离散时间问题，从而利用现有的高效离散时间控制方法。

Method: 1. 使用契约形式化（假设-保证对）描述系统安全边界；2. 将安全控制形式化为契约实现问题；3. 建立垂直层次结构，将实现从连续时间转换到离散时间；4. 构造离散时间系统和相应契约，使得连续时间实现问题的解可以通过离散时间对应问题的解来表征；5. 基于离散时间控制序列构造连续时间控制输入。

Result: 开发了一种方法论，能够将连续时间安全控制问题转化为离散时间问题，并基于离散时间控制序列构造出实现连续时间契约实现的控制输入。

Conclusion: 通过契约形式化和垂直层次结构，成功将连续时间安全控制问题转化为离散时间问题，使得可以利用现有的高效离散时间控制方案来解决原本困难的连续时间安全控制问题。

Abstract: We propose a methodology that exploits the contract formalism to characterize the continuous-time safety control problem, which is often difficult to address, in terms of a discrete-time one, for which numerous efficient solution scheme exist. We construct contracts as pairs of assumptions and guarantees which are set-valued mappings that describe the safe boundaries within which the system must operate. By formalizing safety control as contract implementation, we develop a vertical hierarchy according to which we translate implementation from continuous to discrete time. We accomplish this by constructing a discrete-time system and a contract such that a solution to the continuous-time implementation problem can be characterized in terms of a solution to its discrete-time counterpart. We then use this characterization to construct a control input that establishes implementation in continuous time on the basis of the control sequence that achieves implementation in discrete time.

</details>


### [112] [Optimal Dividend, Reinsurance, and Capital Injection for Collaborating Business Lines under Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25350)
*Tim J. Boonen,Engel John C. Dela Vega,Len Patrick Dominic M. Garces*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了具有两个合作业务线的保险公司在模型不确定性下的最优决策问题，包括分红、再保险和资本注入，得到了闭式解的最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险公司面临模型不确定性时，需要制定稳健策略来平衡分红、再保险和资本注入决策，同时考虑不同业务线的风险偏好差异。

Method: 采用扩散过程建模各业务线的准备金水平，在参考模型和替代模型之间引入扭曲惩罚项，通过最坏情况场景下的鲁棒优化方法求解。

Result: 获得了闭式解的价值函数和最优策略：最优分红-资本注入策略为障碍策略；最优再保险比例和最坏模型偏离随总准备金水平递减。

Conclusion: 模型参数和模糊厌恶程度对最优策略有显著影响，鲁棒方法为保险公司在模型不确定性下的决策提供了理论框架。

Abstract: This paper considers an insurer with two collaborating business lines that faces three critical decisions: (1) dividend payout, (2) reinsurance coverage, and (3) capital injection between the lines, in the presence of model uncertainty. The insurer considers the reference model to be an approximation of the true model, and each line has its own robustness preference. The reserve level of each line is modeled using a diffusion process. The objective is to obtain a robust strategy that maximizes the expected weighted sum of discounted dividends until the first ruin time, while incorporating a penalty term for the distortion between the reference and alternative models in the worst-case scenario. We completely solve this problem and obtain the value function and optimal (equilibrium) strategies in closed form. We show that the optimal dividend-capital injection strategy is a barrier strategy. The optimal proportion of risk ceded to the reinsurer and the deviation of the worst-case model from the reference model are decreasing with respect to the aggregate reserve level. Finally, numerical examples are presented to show the impact of the model parameters and ambiguity aversion on the optimal strategies.

</details>


### [113] [Optimization on Weak Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25396)
*Valentina Zalbertus,Max Pfeffer,Alexander Schmeding*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文发展了弱黎曼流形上的梯度下降优化框架，引入了Hesse流形概念，并建立了形状分析和形状优化中几类弱黎曼流形的优化基础性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维流形上的黎曼结构在形状分析和形状优化中自然出现，这些应用导致在非巴拿赫空间建模的流形上的优化问题，需要建立相应的优化理论框架。

Method: 开发弱黎曼流形上的梯度下降优化基本框架，引入Hesse流形的概念，为形状分析和形状优化相关的几类弱黎曼流形建立优化基础性质。

Result: 建立了弱黎曼流形上梯度下降优化的基本理论框架，定义了Hesse流形概念，并证明了形状分析和形状优化中几类重要弱黎曼流形的优化性质。

Conclusion: 该研究为无限维流形上的优化问题提供了理论基础，特别是在形状分析和形状优化领域，通过引入Hesse流形概念和建立优化框架，填补了非巴拿赫空间建模流形优化理论的空白。

Abstract: Riemannian structures on infinite-dimensional manifolds arise naturally in shape analysis and shape optimization. These applications lead to optimization problems on manifolds which are not modeled on Banach spaces. The present article develops the basic framework for optimization via gradient descent on weak Riemannian manifolds leading to the notion of a Hesse manifold. Further, foundational properties for optimization are established for several classes of weak Riemannian manifolds connected to shape analysis and shape optimization.

</details>


### [114] [High-Resolution Inertial Dynamics with Time-Rescaled Gradients for Nonsmooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25401)
*Manh Hung Le,Andrea Simonetto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于非光滑凸优化的连续时间动力学系统，作为Nesterov加速梯度法的高分辨率ODE扩展，结合时变Moreau包络平滑、时间重标度和Hessian驱动阻尼项，实现快速收敛和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Nesterov加速梯度法(NAG)扩展到非光滑凸优化问题，通过连续时间动力学系统框架理解时变平滑和时间重标度如何共同控制加速和稳定性，为经典加速方法提供更精确的高分辨率模型。

Method: 对适当凸下半连续目标函数应用时变Moreau包络平滑，引入受控时间重标度的梯度项，耦合Hessian驱动阻尼项，构建惯性动力学系统。建立系统的适定性，构造Lyapunov能量函数捕捉惯性、阻尼和平滑的综合效应。将框架扩展到极大单调算子设置。

Result: 在适当标度下，能量耗散导致目标函数和梯度的快速衰减、速度稳定化，在温和假设下轨迹弱收敛到极小值点。系统作为非光滑高分辨率NAG模型，阐明了时变平滑和时间重标度共同控制加速和稳定的机制。数值实验验证了主要参数效应和系统性能。

Conclusion: 提出的连续时间动力学系统为Nesterov加速梯度法提供了非光滑情况下的高分辨率ODE扩展，通过时变Moreau平滑和时间重标度的协同作用实现快速收敛和稳定性，框架可扩展到极大单调算子，为理解加速优化算法提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study nonsmooth convex minimization through a continuous-time dynamical system that can be seen as a high-resolution ODE of Nesterov Accelerated Gradient (NAG) adapted to the nonsmooth case. We apply a time-varying Moreau envelope smoothing to a proper convex lower semicontinuous objective function and introduce a controlled time-rescaling of the gradient, coupled with a Hessian-driven damping term, leading to our proposed inertial dynamic. We provide a well-posedness result for this dynamical system, and construct a Lyapunov energy function capturing the combined effects of inertia, damping, and smoothing. For an appropriate scaling, the energy dissipates and yields fast decay of the objective function and gradient, stabilization of velocities, and weak convergence of trajectories to minimizers under mild assumptions. Conceptually, the system is a nonsmooth high-resolution model of Nesterov's method that clarifies how time-varying smoothing and time rescaling jointly govern acceleration and stability. We further extend the framework to the setting of maximally monotone operators, for which we propose and analyze a corresponding dynamical system and establish analogous convergence results. We also present numerical experiments illustrating the effect of the main parameters and comparing the proposed system with several benchmark dynamics.

</details>


### [115] [Stochastic maximum principle for time-changed forward-backward stochastic control problem with Lévy noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25486)
*Jingwei Chen,Jun Ye,Feng Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文建立了由时间变换的正向-反向随机微分方程（带Lévy噪声）控制的最优控制问题的随机最大值原理，并应用于现金管理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由时间变换的正向-反向随机微分方程（带Lévy噪声）控制的最优控制问题，这类系统通过随机非递减操作时间（α-稳定从属子的逆）来建模捕获事件和次扩散等现象。

Method: 采用对偶变换和凸变分方法，推导出最优性的必要和充分条件，这些条件通过一组新颖的伴随方程表达。

Result: 建立了时间变换的正向-反向随机微分方程（带Lévy噪声）控制的最优控制问题的随机最大值原理，获得了最优性的必要和充分条件。

Conclusion: 理论结果成功应用于解决具有随机递归效用的显式现金管理问题，验证了所提方法的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper establishes a stochastic maximum principle for optimal control problems governed by time-changed forward-backward stochastic differential equations with Lévy noise. The system incorporates a random, non-decreasing operational time (the inverse of an $α$-stable subordinator) to model phenomena like trapping events and subdiffusion. Using a duality transformation and the convex variational method, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for optimality, expressed through a novel set of adjoint equations. Finally, the theoretical results are applied to solve an explicit cash management problem under stochastic recursive utility.

</details>


### [116] [A Framework for Eliminating Paradoxical Orders in European Day-Ahead Electricity Markets through Mixed-Integer Linear Programming Strong Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25490)
*Zhen Wang,Mohammad Reza Hesamzadeh,Shudian Zhao,Jan Kronqvist*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于增广拉格朗日对偶（ALD）的新型电力市场定价机制，解决了整数变量导致的非凸非可微问题，实现了激励相容、无悖论订单且无需额外支付，并通过改进的SAVLR算法保证计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲日前电力市场中整数变量的存在使社会福利最大化问题非凸非可微，导致经典边际定价理论不一致。现有定价机制难以平衡收入充足性与激励相容性，通常依赖歧视性补贴或存在弱对偶问题。

Method: 利用增广拉格朗日对偶（ALD）框架建立混合整数线性规划（MILP）的强对偶性，提出ALD定价机制。通过改进的替代绝对值拉格朗日松弛（SAVLR）方法高效计算精确惩罚系数和最优拉格朗日乘子。

Result: 理论证明ALD机制具有内在激励相容性，使集中调度与个体激励一致且无需额外支付。该价格信号能消除供应侧的悖论拒绝订单（PROs）和悖论接受订单（PAOs）。需求侧引入充分条件保证收入充足性。数值实验验证了ALD机制的经济优越性和改进SAVLR算法的计算可行性。

Conclusion: ALD定价机制为含整数变量的电力市场提供了理论一致、激励相容且无悖论订单的定价方案，通过改进的SAVLR算法实现计算可行性，为欧洲日前电力市场提供了透明且财务一致的结算系统。

Abstract: The presence of integer variables in the European day-ahead electricity market renders the social welfare maximization problem non-convex and non-differentiable, making classical marginal pricing theoretically inconsistent. Existing pricing mechanisms often struggle to balance revenue adequacy with incentive compatibility, typically relying on discriminatory uplift payments or suffering from weak duality. Leveraging the Augmented Lagrangian Duality (ALD) framework, which establishes strong duality for Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), this paper proposes a novel ALD pricing mechanism. We analytically prove that this mechanism is inherently incentive-compatible, aligning centralized dispatch with individual incentives without requiring side payments. Notably, we demonstrate that the ALD price signals intrinsically eliminate Paradoxically Rejected Orders (PROs) and Paradoxically Accepted Orders (PAOs) for supply orders. For the demand side, a sufficient condition is introduced to further guarantee revenue adequacy, resulting in a transparent and financially consistent settlement system. To ensure computational tractability, we modify the Surrogate Absolute-Value Lagrangian Relaxation (SAVLR) method to efficiently compute the exact penalty coefficients and optimal Lagrangian multipliers. Numerical experiments on illustrative examples and the Nordic 12-area electricity market model confirm the superior economic properties of the ALD pricing mechanism and the tractability of the modified SAVLR algorithm.

</details>


### [117] [A Representation Optimization Dichotomy, Lie-Algebraic Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25525)
*Sooraj KC,Vivek Mishra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了李群参数化高斯策略在强化学习中的优化特性，揭示了李代数类型与梯度Lipschitz常数之间的表示-优化二分法：紧致代数具有常数平滑性，而非紧致代数呈现指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李群参数化在强化学习和随机优化中广泛应用（如旋转、刚体变换），但不同李代数类型对优化性能的影响尚不清楚。需要理解李代数结构如何影响梯度平滑性、步长选择和收敛速度，以设计更高效的优化算法。

Method: 建立李群MDP类中李代数参数化高斯策略目标的表示-优化二分法理论框架。分析梯度Lipschitz常数L(R)与李代数类型的关系，推导紧致代数（如so(n), su(n)）和非紧致代数（如gl(n)）的不同性质。提出基于李代数投影的优化算法替代Fisher逆矩阵计算。

Result: 理论证明：紧致李代数具有常数梯度Lipschitz常数L=O(1)，而非紧致代数（如gl(n)）呈现指数增长L=Θ(exp(2R))。算法上，紧致代数的半径无关平滑性支持O(1/√T)收敛，使用O(n²J)李代数投影替代O(d_g³)Fisher逆计算。Kantorovich对齐界α≥2κ/(κ+1)提供了投影近似自然梯度更新的可计算条件。

Conclusion: 李代数类型决定了优化问题的内在性质，形成了表示-优化二分法。紧致代数提供常数平滑性，支持高效优化；非紧致代数呈现指数增长，限制优化性能。提出的李代数投影算法在SO(3)^J和SE(3)实验中验证了理论，相比Cholesky-based Fisher逆计算获得1.1-1.7倍加速，且随规模增大增益增加。

Abstract: Structured reinforcement learning and stochastic optimization often involve parameters evolving on matrix Lie groups such as rotations and rigid-body transformations. We establish a representation-optimization dichotomy for Lie-algebra-parameterized Gaussian policy objectives in the Lie Group MDP class: the gradient Lipschitz constant L(R), governing step size, convergence, and sample complexity of first-order methods, depends only on the algebraic type of g, uniformly over all objectives, independent of reward or transition structure. Specifically, L = O(1) for compact g (e.g., so(n), su(n)), and L = Theta(exp(2R)) for g = gl(n), with O(exp(2R)) for all algebras with a hyperbolic element. A key lower bound shows this exponential growth cannot be canceled by interaction between the exponential map and the objective, making the dichotomy intrinsic to the algebra.This yields an algorithmic consequence: for compact algebras, radius-independent smoothness enables O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence using an O(n^2 J) Lie-algebraic projection step instead of O(d_g^3) Fisher inversion. A Kantorovich alignment bound alpha >= 2 kappa / (kappa + 1) provides a computable condition under which this projection approximates natural gradient updates. Experiments on SO(3)^J and SE(3) confirm the theory: constant smoothness for compact algebras, polynomial growth for SE(3), and alignment across condition regimes. The projection step achieves 1.1-1.7x speedup over Cholesky-based Fisher inversion, with increasing gains at larger scales.

</details>


### [118] [Particle method for a nonlinear multimarginal optimal transport problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25584)
*Adrien Cances,Quentin Mérigot,Luca Nenna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于非线性多边际最优传输问题的拉格朗日粒子离散化方法，通过点云近似耦合并使用Wasserstein惩罚强制边际约束，证明了当粒子数趋于无穷时的定量收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险管理中的非线性多边际最优传输问题虽然可以通过添加额外边际转化为线性问题，但需要有效的数值计算方法。现有方法在处理高维问题时面临挑战，因此需要开发基于粒子离散化的高效算法。

Method: 提出拉格朗日粒子离散化方法：1) 使用均匀加权的点云近似可容许耦合；2) 通过Wasserstein惩罚强制边际约束；3) 分析收敛性，收敛速率由最优解的均匀量化误差决定，可用其支撑集的几何特性（如盒维数）界定。

Result: 证明了粒子离散化方法的定量收敛结果：收敛速率由最优解的均匀量化误差决定。对于单变量边际和超模成本函数（此时最优耦合为共单调），获得了更尖锐的收敛速率，可用边际本身的渐近量化误差表示。特别地，对于条件风险价值（CVaR），问题简化为多边际部分传输公式。

Conclusion: 提出的拉格朗日粒子离散化方法为非线性多边际最优传输问题提供了有效的数值计算框架，具有理论收敛保证。该方法在风险管理、部分重心计算等领域具有应用价值，并通过数值实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We study a nonlinear multimarginal optimal transport problem arising in risk management, where the objective is to maximize a spectral risk measure of the pushforward of a coupling by a cost function. Although this problem is inherently nonlinear, it is known to have an equivalent linear reformulation as a multimarginal transport problem with an additional marginal. We introduce a Lagrangian particle discretization of this problem, in which admissible couplings are approximated by uniformly weighted point clouds, and marginal constraints are enforced through Wasserstein penalization. We prove quantitative convergence results for this discretization as the number of particles tends to infinity. The convergence rate is shown to be governed by the uniform quantization error of an optimal solution, and can be bounded in terms of the geometric properties of its support, notably its box dimension. In the case of univariate marginals and supermodular cost functions, where optimal couplings are known to be comonotone, we obtain sharper convergence rates expressed in terms of the asymptotic quantization errors of the marginals themselves. We also discuss the particular case of conditional value at risk, for which the problem reduces to a multimarginal partial transport formulation. Finally, we illustrate our approach with numerical experiments in several application domains, including risk management and partial barycenters, as well as some artificial examples with a repulsive cost.

</details>


### [119] [Instance-optimal stochastic convex optimization: Can we improve upon sample-average and robust stochastic approximation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25657)
*Liwei Jiang,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: VISOR是一种方差缩减方法，在具有加性和乘性噪声的随机优化问题中显著优于传统方法，达到实例最优的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在具有加性和乘性噪声的随机优化问题中，传统方法（如样本平均逼近和鲁棒随机逼近）在有限样本下表现不佳，需要开发更有效的算法。

Method: 提出VISOR（方差缩减策略），特别是其加速变体，通过精心设计的方差控制机制来处理随机优化中的噪声问题。

Result: VISOR在相同样本量下显著优于传统方法，达到实例最优的样本复杂度（至多对数因子），并在广义线性模型中改进了经典结果。

Conclusion: VISOR是处理具有加性和乘性噪声的随机优化问题的有效方法，在有限样本下达到理论最优性能，为广义线性模型等应用提供了最佳的非渐近、实例依赖泛化误差界。

Abstract: We study the unconstrained minimization of a smooth and strongly convex population loss function under a stochastic oracle that introduces both additive and multiplicative noise; this is a canonical and widely-studied setting that arises across operations research, signal processing, and machine learning. We begin by showing that standard approaches such as sample average approximation and robust (or averaged) stochastic approximation can lead to suboptimal -- and in some cases arbitrarily poor -- performance with realistic finite sample sizes. In contrast, we demonstrate that a carefully designed variance reduction strategy, which we term VISOR for short, can significantly outperform these approaches while using the same sample size. Our upper bounds are complemented by finite-sample, information-theoretic local minimax lower bounds, which highlight fundamental, instance-dependent factors that govern the performance of any estimator. Taken together, these results demonstrate that an accelerated variant of VISOR is instance-optimal, achieving the best possible sample complexity up to logarithmic factors while also attaining optimal oracle complexity. We apply our theory to generalized linear models and improve upon classical results. In particular, we obtain the best-known non-asymptotic, instance-dependent generalization error bounds for stochastic methods, even in linear regression.

</details>


### [120] [Atomic Gradient Flows: Gradient Flows on Sparse Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25675)
*Christian Amend,Marcello Carioni,Konstantinos Zemas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将粒子梯度流(PGFs)推广到任意巴拿赫空间中的凸优化问题，提出了原子梯度流(AGFs)方法，通过正则化子单位球的极值点作为"粒子"，在稀疏表示下建立度量梯度流，并证明其在Wasserstein空间中的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粒子梯度流(PGFs)方法仅适用于测度空间中的总变差正则化问题，限制了其应用范围。作者希望将这一高效方法推广到更一般的巴拿赫空间凸优化问题中，利用稀疏表示降低计算成本。

Method: 1. 将正则化子单位球的极值点定义为"原子"（粒子），基于Krein-Milman定理保证逼近能力；2. 在稀疏表示上定义离散化泛函，通过Γ-收敛证明与原问题的一致性；3. 使用最小化运动方案建立演化过程，证明λ-凸性和唯一性；4. 利用Choquet定理将问题提升到权重和极值点的Wasserstein空间；5. 证明AGF演化在Wasserstein空间中也是度量梯度流。

Result: 1. 成功将PGFs推广到任意巴拿赫空间，提出了AGFs框架；2. 建立了稀疏表示下梯度流的数学理论基础，包括存在性、唯一性和收敛性；3. 证明了AGF演化在Wasserstein空间中的一致性；4. 展示了方法在有界变差函数优化和最优传输正则化测度曲线等无限维问题中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 原子梯度流(AGFs)为巴拿赫空间中的凸优化问题提供了一个统一的稀疏表示框架，将粒子梯度流的计算优势扩展到更广泛的问题类别，同时保持了与Wasserstein空间动态的一致性，为无限维优化问题提供了高效的计算方法。

Abstract: One of the most popular approaches for solving total variation-regularized optimization problems in the space of measures are Particle Gradient Flows (PGFs). These restrict the problem to linear combinations of Dirac deltas and then perform a Euclidean gradient flow in the weights and positions, significantly reducing the computational cost while still decreasing the energy. In this work, we generalize PGFs to convex optimization problems in arbitrary Banach spaces, which we call Atomic Gradient Flows (AGFs).
  To this end, the crucial ingredient turns out to be the right notion of particles, chosen here as the extremal points of the unit ball of the regularizer. This choice is motivated by the Krein-Milman theorem, which ensures that minimizers can be approximated by linear combinations of extremal points. We investigate metric gradient flows of the optimization problem when restricted to such sparse representations, for which we define a suitable discretized functional that we show to be to be consistent with the original problem via the means of $Γ$-convergence. We prove that the resulting evolution of the latter is well-defined using a minimizing movement scheme, and we establish conditions ensuring $λ$-convexity and uniqueness of the flow.
  Then, using Choquet's theorem, we lift the problem into the Wasserstein space on weights and extremal points, and consider Wasserstein gradient flows in this lifted setting. Our main result is that the lifting of the AGF evolution is again a metric gradient flow in the Wasserstein space, verifying the consistency of the approach with respect to a Wasserstein-type dynamic.
  Finally, we illustrate the applicability of AGFs to several relevant infinite-dimensional problems, including optimization of functions of bounded variation and curves of measures regularized by Optimal Transport-type penalties.

</details>
